\leq\;&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial_{t}b^{\varepsilon}{\mathrm{d}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\left[\nabla_{x}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})+\varepsilon\mathcal{X}_{b^{\varepsilon}}\right]\right|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq\;&-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\varepsilon\Big(|\mathcal{X}_{c^{\varepsilon}}|+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}|\nabla_{x}\cdot b^{\varepsilon}|\Big)|{b^{\varepsilon}}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\;&+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{x}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})\right|{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|\varepsilon(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})b^{\varepsilon}\cdot\mathcal{X}_{b^{\varepsilon}}\right|{\mathrm{d}}x\\
\leq\;&-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+C\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t),\end{split}

where the fourth inequality above is derived from the integrating by parts with respect to
t
.
Further, thanks to the Hölder inequality, the Sobolev embedding inequality and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\mathcal{X}_{2,2}+\mathcal{X}_{2,3}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x}^{3}}\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}+\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

where here and below
{\eta}>0
is a sufficiently small constant.
Here we have used the Nirenberg inequality

\|f\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}f\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla_{x}^{2}f\|_{L_{x}^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{x}f\|_{H_{x}^{1}}.

(2.23)

Therefore, gathering the above bounds of
\mathcal{X}_{2,1}
,
\mathcal{X}_{2,2}
and
\mathcal{X}_{2,3}
together yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{2}\leq\;&-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}+c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+C\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\\
\;&+C\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\frac{C\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}.\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{X}_{3}
,
the collision symmetry of
\Gamma
and (
A.3
) implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{3}=\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\lesssim&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim&\;\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Consequently, putting the above estimates of
\mathcal{X}_{1}
,
\mathcal{X}_{2}
and
\mathcal{X}_{3}
into (
2.22
) and choosing
\eta
small enough, we conclude

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[\left\|f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\|E^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|B^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right]+\varepsilon\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(a^{\varepsilon}\!+\!c^{\varepsilon})|b^{\varepsilon}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.24)

Step 2.
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of the pure space derivative
\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}

(1\leq|\alpha|\leq N).

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{x}
with
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N
to the first equation of (
1.2
) and taking the inner product with
\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}

\times

\mathbb{R}_{v}^{3}
, we obtain by employing the coercivity of
L
in (
1.11
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\right]+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\underbrace{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\sum_{0\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\!\!\!\Big\langle v\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{4}}+\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\!\!\!\Big\langle\varepsilon\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{5}}\\
&+\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\times\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{6}}+\underbrace{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{7}}.\end{split}

(2.25)

For the term
{\mathcal{X}_{4}}
, recalling the macro-micro decomposition (
2.3
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{4}=\;&\underbrace{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\sum_{0\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{4,1}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\sum_{0\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{4,2}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\sum_{0\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{4,3}}.\end{split}

Utilizing the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
) and the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{4,1}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\left(\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Applying the analogous argument of the estimate of
\mathcal{X}_{4,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{4,2}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{X}_{4,3}
, we deduce from
the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
), the Sobolev embeddings
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
\hookrightarrow L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{4,3}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{0\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\\
\;&\quad+\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

Via the summarization of the bounds of
\mathcal{X}_{4,i}(i=1,2,3)
, we find

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{4}\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}.\end{split}

For the term
{\mathcal{X}_{5}}
, utilizing (
2.3
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{5}=\;&\underbrace{\!\!\!\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\!\!\!\Big\langle\varepsilon\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{5,1}}\underbrace{\!-\!\!\!\!\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\!\!\!\left\langle\varepsilon\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{5,2}}.\end{split}

It is derived from the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
), the Sobolev embeddings
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, (
2.3
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{5,1}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\cdot\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\cdot\|\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\\
\;&\quad+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\cdot\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\left(\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}\left(\|\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\|\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Similarly, the quantity
\mathcal{X}_{5,2}
is controlled by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{5,2}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\cdot\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\cdot\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Thus, collecting the above bounds of
\mathcal{X}_{5,1}
and
\mathcal{X}_{5,2}
shows us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{5}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{4}\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\|\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

For the term
{\mathcal{X}_{6}}
, using (
2.3
) gives rise to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{6}=\;&\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\times\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{X}_{6,1}}\\
\;&\underbrace{-\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle v\times\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon},\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.}_{\mathcal{X}_{6,2}}\end{split}

Applying the analogous arguments of the estimates of
{\mathcal{X}_{5,1}}
and
{\mathcal{X}_{5,2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{6,1}\lesssim\;&\Big[\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 2}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\\
\;&+\sum_{3\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\cdot\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\!\left(\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}\left(\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t),\end{split}

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{6,2}\lesssim\;&\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

As a result, collecting the above estimates of
\mathcal{X}_{6,1}
and
\mathcal{X}_{6,2}
leads to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{6}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{X}_{7}
,
employing (
A.3
) and the Sobolev embeddings theory implies

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{X}_{7}\lesssim&\;\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big[\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}(\nu)}\|\partial^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\\
&\;\quad+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\|f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Consequently, substituting all the estimates of
\mathcal{X}_{4}\sim\mathcal{X}_{7}

into (
2.25
) and choosing
\eta
small enough and then summing the resulting inequality over
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\Big[\!\!\!\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\!\!\!\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\!\!\!\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\!\!\!\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(E^{\varepsilon},B^{\varepsilon})\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\Big]+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\\
\lesssim\;&\big[\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\big]^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\left(\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)\\
\;&+\!\varepsilon^{3}\|\nabla_{x}[E^{\varepsilon},B^{\varepsilon}]\|_{H^{2}_{x}}^{2}\Big(\varepsilon\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{x}^{N}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}^{N-1}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big).\end{split}

(2.26)

In summary, adding
(
2.24
) and (
2.26
) together and employing the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) lead to
(
2.21
). This completes the proof of Lemma
2.3
.
∎

With Lemma
2.2
and Lemma
2.3
in hand, we are now in the position to complete the proof of Proposition
2.1
.

Proof of Proposition
2.1
.

Choosing
0<\eta_{1}\ll 1
and
\delta_{0}
small enough and taking the proper linear combination of (
2.6
)
\times\eta_{1}+
(
2.21
) induce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\;&+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\lesssim\delta_{0}\left(\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\right)\\
\;&+\frac{\delta_{0}}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\Big(\varepsilon\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{x}^{N}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}^{N-1}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big),\end{split}

(2.27)

where
{\mathcal{E}}_{N}(t)
comes from the energy given in (
2.18
) and (
2.21
), as follows

\displaystyle\begin{split}{\mathcal{E}}_{N}(t):=\;&\left\|f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\left\|E^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\left\|B^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\eta_{1}\varepsilon{\mathcal{E}}_{int}^{N}(t).\end{split}

(2.28)

By referring back to (
1.16
),
(
1.19
)
and (
1.24
), we can easily find that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\left\|\langle v\rangle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\leq\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,4N}(t)\leq{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t),\\
&\varepsilon\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{x}^{N}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}}_{N,l_{2}}(t),\\
&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\langle v\rangle\nabla_{v}\nabla_{x}^{N-1}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\leq\varepsilon(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}{\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}}_{N,l_{2}}(t).\end{split}

(2.29)

Hence, putting (
2.29
) into (
2.27
) yields the desired estimate (
2.5
).
This completes the proof of Proposition
2.1
.
∎

2.2.
Weighted Energy Estimate

In this subsection, we establish the energy estimates for the VMB system (
1.2
) with different weight functions
\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)
and
\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)
.
First of all, we establish the following the energy estimate with the weight
\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)
for
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1
.

Proposition 2.4
.

Let
N,l_{1},l_{2}
be fixed parameters stated in Theorem
1.1
.
Assume that
(f^{\varepsilon},E^{\varepsilon},B^{\varepsilon})
is a solution to the VMB system (
1.2
) defined on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}

and the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) holds true for
\delta_{0}
small enough. Then, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}(1+t)^{-{\frac{1+s}{2}}(l_{2}-l_{1})}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1,|\alpha|\geq 1}}\!\!\!\!\big\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+(1+\delta_{0})\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

(2.30)

Here,
{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)
and
{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)
are defined in (
1.15
) and (
1.16
), respectively.

Proof of Proposition
2.4
.

Applying microscopic projection
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})
to the first equation in (
1.2
) and using
(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})(v\sqrt{\mu}\cdot E^{\varepsilon})=0
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\;\partial_{t}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}L((\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon})\\
=&\;-(\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}+v\times B^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot E^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon})+[[\mathbf{P},\mathcal{A}]]f^{\varepsilon}.\end{split}

(2.31)

Here
[[\mathbf{P},\mathcal{A}]]:=\mathbf{P}\mathcal{A}-\mathcal{A}\mathbf{P}
denotes the commutator of two operators
\mathbf{P}
and
\mathcal{A}
given by

\mathcal{A}:=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}v\cdot\nabla_{x}+(\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}+v\times B^{\varepsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}-\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot E^{\varepsilon}.

Taking
\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}
with
|\beta|=m
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1
to (
2.31
) and then integrating the resulting identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by multiplying
\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\;\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta^{\prime})\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{C_{\eta}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
&\;+\Big\langle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}C_{\beta}^{e_{i}}\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha+e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\;+\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(-v\times B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\;+\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\;+\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot E^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
&\;\!+\!\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\!\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\!\!\!\!\!\!\!+\!\Big\langle\!\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}[[\mathbf{P},\mathcal{A}]]f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}.\end{split}

(2.32)

Here, we have used the spectral inequality (
A.2
).
Now we classify the last six terms on the right-hand side of (
2.32
) as
\mathcal{Y}_{1}
to
\mathcal{Y}_{6}
and estimate them term by term.
The Hölder inequality and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
give

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{1}\lesssim\;&C_{\eta}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha+e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2},\end{split}

where we have used the fact
\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})=\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)
.

For the term
\mathcal{Y}_{2}
, direct calculation shows us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{2}=\;&\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle-v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta_{1}|=1}\Big\langle-\partial_{\beta_{1}}v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\equiv\;&\mathcal{Y}_{2,1}+\mathcal{Y}_{2,2}.\end{split}

Applying the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
) and the Sobolev embedding inequality,
we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{2,1}\lesssim\;&\Big[\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\;+\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,2\}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\;+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\eta{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

(2.33)

Here, we have used the facts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\quad\text{in the case of}\quad 2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,2\},\\
\;&\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(1,|\beta|+1)\quad\quad\;\quad\;\;\text{in the case of}\quad|\alpha|\geq 3.\end{split}

Furthermore, it is derived from
l_{2}-l_{1}=\frac{N-1}{2}:=\widetilde{\ell}
and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\Big[(\varepsilon^{2-\varrho}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})^{\frac{2}{\varrho}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\widetilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big]^{\varrho}\\
\;&\times\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\Big]^{1-\varrho}\\
\lesssim\;&(\varepsilon^{2-\varrho}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})^{\frac{2}{\varrho}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\widetilde{\ell}}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&(\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})^{\frac{2}{\varrho}}\!\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\!\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\eta{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t),\end{split}

(2.34)

where we have taken
\varrho
as

\varrho={\widetilde{\ell}}^{-1}.

Putting the bound (
2.34
) into (
2.33
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{2,1}\lesssim\;&(\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})^{\frac{2}{\varrho}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\eta{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{Y}_{2,2}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{2,2}\lesssim\;&\!\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta_{1}|=1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\!\!B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime},\beta)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta-\beta_{1}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\end{split}

by the Hölder inequality and (
2.3
).
Here, we also have used the fact
|\alpha|\leq N-2
because of
|\beta|\geq 1
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1
in this case.
As a result, collecting the above bounds
\mathcal{Y}_{2,1}
and
\mathcal{Y}_{2,2}
and utilizing the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) lead to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{2}\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+s}{2}\widetilde{\ell}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha\!-\!\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+(\eta\!+\!\delta_{0}){\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{Y}_{3}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{3}=\;&\Big\langle-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\equiv\;&\mathcal{Y}_{3,2}+\mathcal{Y}_{3,2}.\end{split}

Notice that

\nabla_{v}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)=(l_{1}-|\alpha|-|\beta|)\langle v\rangle^{l_{1}-|\alpha|-|\beta|-1}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\frac{v}{|v|}+\langle v\rangle^{l_{1}-|\alpha|-|\beta|}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}{\frac{2q}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}\langle v\rangle,

which means
\nabla_{v}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)
,
and hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{3,1}=\;&\Big\langle-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)}{\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\overline{w}^{2}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

Taking the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory gives rise to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{3,2}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,1\}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\quad+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{3}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t),\end{split}

where we have used the facts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\quad\,\text{in the case of}\quad 1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,1\},\\
\;&\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\overline{w}_{l_{1}}(1,|\beta|+1)\quad\quad\qquad\text{in the case of}\quad|\alpha|\geq 2.\end{split}

Thus, the above estimates of
\mathcal{Y}_{3,1}
and
\mathcal{Y}_{3,2}

and the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) give us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{3}\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\ \lesssim\delta_{0}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

By employing the similar argument in the estimate of
\mathcal{Y}_{2}
, we deduce from the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{4}\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\varepsilon^{3}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\ \lesssim\delta_{0}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

Further, employing (
A.4
), the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) and the Hölder inequality yields

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{5}\lesssim\sqrt{{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\lesssim\delta_{0}{\overline{\mathcal{D}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t).\end{split}

Owing to the Maxwellian structure for the macro projection
\mathbf{P}
defined in (
2.4
), we directly deduce from
the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{Y}_{6}\lesssim\;&\Big[\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\left(\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}+\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}\right)\left(\|\nabla_{x}\nabla_{v}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\\
\;&+\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}}\left(\|\nabla_{x}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\|\nabla_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\right)\Big]\|\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\varepsilon\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Therefore, plugging all the estimates of
\mathcal{Y}_{1}\sim\mathcal{Y}_{6}

into (
2.32
),
taking the summation over
\left\{|\beta|=m,|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N-1\right\}
for each given
0\leq m\leq N-1
, and then taking combination of those
N-1
estimates with properly chosen constant
C_{m}>0

(0\leq m\leq N-1)
and
\eta
,
\delta_{0}
small enough,
we obtain
(
2.30
). This completes the proof of Proposition
2.4
.
∎

Next, we turn to establishing the weighted energy estimate with the weight
\overline{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)
as follows.

Proposition 2.5
.

Let
N,l_{2}
be fixed parameters stated in Theorem
1.1
.
Assume that the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) holds true for
\delta_{0}
small enough.
Then, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)\lesssim\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\eta\varepsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-(1+\vartheta)}\|\nabla_{x}^{N}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.35)

Here,
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)
are defined in (
1.18
) and (
1.19
), respectively.

To prove Proposition
2.5
, the construction of

\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)
given in (
1.18
) heavily depends on the different order weighted energy estimate of

\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)
matched with different order time decay factor.
Therefore, to make it clear, we introduce the
n
-th weighted
instant energy functional
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{l_{2}}^{(n)}(t)
of
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)
and the corresponding dissipation functional
\widetilde{{\mathcal{D}}}_{l_{2}}^{(n)}(t)
of
\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)

defined as

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{l_{2}}^{(n)}(t):=\sum_{0\leq j\leq n}{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{n,j}(t),\quad\widetilde{{\mathcal{D}}}_{l_{2}}^{(n)}(t):=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{{0\leq j\leq n}}{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{n,j}(t)\quad\text{for }\;0\leq n\leq N.

(2.36)

Here, in the case of
0\leq n\leq N-1
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{n,j}(t)
and
{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{n,j}(t)
are given by

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{n,j}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,j}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n,|\beta|=j}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\quad\text{for }\;0\leq j\leq n,

\displaystyle{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{{n,j}}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,j}}\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n,|\beta|=j}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,j}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n,|\beta|=j}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\quad\text{for}\;0\leq j\leq n,

and in the case of
n=N
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{N,j}(t)
and
{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{N,j}(t)
are given by

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{E}}}_{l_{2}}^{{N,j}}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,j}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,|\beta|=j}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\quad\quad\quad\quad\text{for }\;1\leq j\leq N,

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{E}}}_{l_{2}}^{{N,0}}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\varepsilon\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}},

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{D}}}_{l_{2}}^{{N,j}}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,j}}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,|\beta|=j}\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,j}}\!\!\!\!\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,|\beta|=j}\frac{1}{\varepsilon^{2}}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}\;\quad\text{for}\;1\leq j\leq N,

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{D}}}_{l_{2}}^{{N,0}}(t):=\;

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\frac{\varepsilon q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\sum_{|\alpha|=N}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}

with
\sigma_{n,|\beta|}
given by

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\sigma_{n,0}=0,\qquad\qquad\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}=\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}\qquad\text{for }\;n\leq N-1,\\
\;&\sigma_{N,0}=\frac{1+\vartheta}{2},\quad\;\;\sigma_{N,|\beta|+1}-\sigma_{N,|\beta|}=\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}.\end{split}

(2.37)

Then we provide the weighted energy estimate with the weight function
\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)
for
|\alpha|+|\beta|=N
as follows.

Lemma 2.6
.

Assume that the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) holds true for
\delta_{0}
small enough.
Then, there holds

\displaystyle\begin{split}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{(N)}(t)+\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{(N)}(t)\lesssim\delta_{0}\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{N,l_{2}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\eta\varepsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-(1+\vartheta)}\|\nabla_{x}^{N}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}.\end{split}

(2.38)

Here,
\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{l_{2}}^{(N)}(t)
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}_{l_{2}}^{(N)}(t)
are defined in (
2.36
).

Proof.

Our proof of (
2.38
) is divided into
two steps as follows.

Step 1. Weighted
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of
\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}
with the pure spatial derivative
|\alpha|=N
.

More precisely, we can establish

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{E}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)\right]+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta\varepsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,0}(t)\\
\;&+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t).\end{split}

(2.39)

In fact,
applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{x}
with
|\alpha|=N
to the first equation in (
1.2
), then integrating the resulting identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by multiplying

\varepsilon w^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\!\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\!0)\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\!f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\!+\!\frac{\varepsilon q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\left\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}_{x}\!f^{\varepsilon}\right\|^{2}\!+\!\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon}\left\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\right\|_{{\nu}}^{2}\\
=&{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(E^{\varepsilon}\cdot v\sqrt{\mu}),\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}+{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\left(-v\times B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
&\;+{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\left(-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\varepsilon}\right),\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}+{\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Big(\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot E^{\varepsilon}f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
&\;+{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}+\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\right\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}\!(\nu)}^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

(2.40)

Here, we have used the spectral inequality (
A.1
).
Now we classify the first five terms on the right-hand side of (
2.32
) as
\mathcal{J}_{1}
to
\mathcal{J}_{5}
and estimate them term by term.
For the term
\mathcal{J}_{1}
, it is derived from the Hölder inequality and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{1}\lesssim\;\varepsilon\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\lesssim\;\eta\varepsilon^{2}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N,0}}\|\nabla^{N}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

From the macro-micro decomposition (
2.3
), we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{2}=\;&-\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\;&-\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\[0.28453pt]
\;&-\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}\\
\equiv\;&\mathcal{J}_{2,1}+\mathcal{J}_{2,2}+\mathcal{J}_{2,3}.\end{split}

From the Hölder inequality, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
, the interpolation inequality, (
2.23
) and the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
,
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{6}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we deduce

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{2,1}\lesssim\;&{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big[\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-1,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{N-1}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=2}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-2,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=3}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-2,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\\
\;&\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{4\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-2,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{3}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-1,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{N-1}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{5}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\delta_{0}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,1}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t).\end{split}

Here, we have used (
2.2
) and the facts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\lesssim\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-1,1),\quad\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N,0)\lesssim\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(N-2,1)\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{7}{2}}.\end{split}

The terms
\mathcal{J}_{2,2}
and
\mathcal{J}_{2,3}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\mathcal{J}_{2,2}+\mathcal{J}_{2,3}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\!\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\!\!\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\!+\!\!\!\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\!\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\!\Big]\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\!\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\Big[\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\|\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\!\!+\!\!\!\!\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\end{split}

by making use of the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory.
Therefore, collecting the previous estimates of the terms
\mathcal{J}_{2,1}
,
\mathcal{J}_{2,2}
and
\mathcal{J}_{2,3}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{2}\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t)+\delta_{0}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Direct calculation gives us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{3}=\;&\underbrace{\Big\langle-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon},\varepsilon\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{3,1}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle-\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{3,2}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle-\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{3,3}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\Big\langle\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon},\nabla_{v}\left(\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\right)\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{3,4}}.\end{split}

Notice that
\nabla_{v}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\lesssim\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)
,
and hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{3,1}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\Big[\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\!+\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\Big]\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\varepsilon^{2}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Then we deduce from the Hölder inequality, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
and the Sobolev embedding (
2.23
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{3,2}\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\!0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{5}\!\!\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq 2}\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\!E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha\!-\!\alpha^{\prime}|,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\|_{L^{2}_{x,\!v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{5}\!\!\!\sum_{3\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\!\!\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{2}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|+2,1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{7}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\varepsilon^{7}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t).\end{split}

Via the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev embedding theory, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\mathcal{J}_{3,3}+\mathcal{J}_{3,4}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\Big[\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{N-1}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{2}\Big[\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{N-1}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}+\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq N}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}\mathbf{P}f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Therefore, collecting the estimates on the quantities

\mathcal{J}_{3,i}(i=1,2,3,4)
in the previous and using the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{3}\lesssim\;&(\eta+\delta_{0})(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,1}(t)+\delta_{0}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

Applying the analogous argument for the term
\mathcal{J}_{2}
, we can deduce that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{4}\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

From (
A.4
), the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) and the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{5}\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,0)\partial_{x}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}(\nu)}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}{\overline{\mathcal{E}}}_{N-1,l_{1}}(t)\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,0}(t)+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}_{N}(t)}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,0}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,0}(t)+\delta_{0}\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

In summary, putting all the estimates of
\mathcal{J}_{1}\sim\mathcal{J}_{5}

into (
2.40
) and choosing
\eta
small enough, (
2.39
) follows.

Step 2. Weighted
L_{x,v}^{2}
-estimate of
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\varepsilon}
with the mixed derivative
|\alpha|+|\beta|=N
,
|\beta|=m\geq 1
.

To be more precise, we can obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t)\right]+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t)+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|-1}(t)+\delta_{0}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}(t)\\
\;&+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|-1}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{N}(t).\end{split}

(2.41)

Indeed, applying
\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}
with
|\alpha|+|\beta|=N,|\beta|=m\geq 1
to (
2.31
) and then integrating the resulting identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by multiplying
\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}}^{2}\\
\;&+\frac{\sigma_{0}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
\leq&\;\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|\leq|\beta|}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta^{\prime})\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}+\frac{C_{\sigma_{0}}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L_{x,v}^{2}(\nu)}^{2}\\
&\;+{\Big\langle-\frac{1}{\varepsilon}C_{\beta}^{e_{i}}\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha+e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
&\;+{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(-v\times B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
&\;+{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Big(-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot E^{\varepsilon}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big),\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
&+{\Big\langle\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\Gamma(f^{\varepsilon},f^{\varepsilon}),\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}\\
\;&+{\Big\langle\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\big([[\mathbf{P},\mathcal{A}_{(E^{\varepsilon},B^{\varepsilon})}]]f^{\varepsilon}\big),\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}.}\end{split}

(2.42)

Here, we have used the spectral inequality (
A.2
).
Now we classify the last five terms on the
right-hand side of (
2.42
) as
\mathcal{J}_{6}
to
\mathcal{J}_{10}
and estimate them term by term.
The Hölder inequality, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality with
\eta
and the interpolation inequality lead to

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{6}\lesssim\;&\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha+e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}+C_{\eta}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha+e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t)+C_{\eta}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|-1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|-1}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t)+(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|-1}(t),\end{split}

where we have used (
2.37
) and the fact that
\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)=\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha+e_{i},\beta-e_{i})\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}
.

For the term
\mathcal{J}_{7}
, we split it into

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{7}=\;&\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|,|\beta^{\prime}|=1}\Big\langle-\partial_{\beta_{1}}v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle-v\times\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\equiv\;&\mathcal{J}_{7,1}+\mathcal{J}_{7,2}.\end{split}

For
\mathcal{J}_{7,1}
, we deduce by the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
) and the
a priori
assumption (
2.2
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{7,1}\lesssim\;&\Big[\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|-1,|\beta^{\prime}|=1}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime},\beta)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\quad+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta|)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta-\beta^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t).\end{split}

Here, we have used the fact
|\alpha|\leq N-1
because of
|\beta|\geq 1
and
|\alpha|+|\beta|=N
in this case.
It is worth pointing out that the most subtle part is the estimate of
\mathcal{J}_{7,2}
which is carried out as

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{7,2}\lesssim\;&\Big[\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\;\;+\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,2\}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\;\;+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,\beta+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}B^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{4}\|B^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}(1\!+\!t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}\!(t)\!+\!\delta_{0}\!\!\!\sum_{n\leq N-1}\!\!\!(1\!+\!t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}\!(t)\!+\!\eta(1\!+\!t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}\!(t).\end{split}

(2.43)

Here, we have used (
2.2
) and the fact, which is derived from the interpolation inequality and (
2.37
), that

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\!\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\!\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|\!-1\!,\!|\beta|\!+\!1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|\!-1\!,\!|\beta|\!+\!1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\varepsilon(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim\;

\displaystyle\varepsilon(1+t)^{{1+\vartheta}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}(t).

Consequently, collecting the above bounds on the quantities
\mathcal{J}_{7,1}
and
\mathcal{J}_{7,2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{7}\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t).\end{split}

For the term
\mathcal{J}_{8}
, direct calculation gives us that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{8}=\;&\underbrace{\Big\langle-\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{8,1}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle-\varepsilon\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{8,2}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle\frac{\varepsilon}{2}v\cdot\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{8,3}}\\
\;&+\underbrace{\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq|\alpha|}\Big\langle\frac{\varepsilon}{2}\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}_{i}\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L_{x,v}^{2}}}_{\mathcal{J}_{8,4}}.\end{split}

Notice that

\nabla_{v}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)
,
and hence, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{8,1}=\;&\Big\langle\varepsilon E^{\varepsilon}\cdot\frac{\nabla_{v}\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)}{\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon},\widetilde{w}^{2}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\Big\rangle_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t).\end{split}

Applying the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
), the Sobolev embedding
H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\hookrightarrow L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, we derive

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{8,2}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\!\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\!\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|\!-\!1,|\beta|\!+\!1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha\!-\!\alpha^{\prime}}\!(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\!\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}\!-\!\mathbf{P})\!f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&+\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{2\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,2\}}\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha^{\prime}}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\qquad+\|\partial_{x}^{\alpha}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|=1}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha|-1,|\beta|+1)\nabla_{v}\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\;&+\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}^{2}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}(t)+\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}(t)\\
\;&+\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}\!(t)\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|+1}\!(t)+\delta_{0}\!\!\sum_{n\leq N-1}\!\!(1\!+\!t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|+1}\!(t)+\eta(1\!+\!t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}\!(t),\end{split}

where we have used the facts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(|\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}|,|\beta|+1)\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{1}{2}}\quad\text{in the case of}\quad 1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,2\},\\
\;&\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta|+1)\quad\quad\quad\quad\qquad\;\text{in the case of}\quad|\alpha|\geq 3.\end{split}

Then we obtain from the Hölder inequality, (
2.23
), the Sobolev embeddings theory that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{8,3}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\|E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\quad+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,1\}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime},\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\quad+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta|)\partial_{\beta}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}(1+t)^{\frac{1+\vartheta}{2}}(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t)+\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\!\!\!\!\sum_{n\leq N-1}\!\!\!(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\!\!\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t)\\
\;&+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
\lesssim\;&\delta_{0}\sum_{n\leq N}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{\sigma_{N,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{N,|\beta|}(t),\end{split}

where we have used
|\alpha|\leq N-1
and the facts that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\;&\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime},\beta)\quad\;\text{in the case of}\quad 1\leq|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,1\},\\
\;&\langle v\rangle\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\lesssim\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta|)\quad\quad\quad\text{in the case of}\quad|\alpha|\geq 2.\end{split}

For
\mathcal{J}_{8,4}
, thanks to
|\alpha|\leq N-1
in this case, we derive from the Hölder inequality and (
2.23
) that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathcal{J}_{8,4}\lesssim\;&\varepsilon\Big[\sum_{|\alpha^{\prime}|\leq\max\{|\alpha|-1,1\}}\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha-\alpha^{\prime},\beta-e_{i})\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}^{\alpha-\alpha^{\prime}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\;&\quad+\|\partial^{\alpha}_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(1,|\beta-e_{i}|)\partial_{\beta-e_{i}}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\Big]\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}\\
\lesssim\;&\varepsilon^{4}\|\nabla_{x}E^{\varepsilon}\|_{H^{N-1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{n\leq N-1}(1+t)^{\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}}\mathcal{D}_{l_{2}}^{n,|\beta|-1}(t)+\frac{\eta}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\widetilde{w}_{l_{2}}(\alpha,\beta)\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{P})f^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{2}_{x,v}}^{2}\\
